<div class="overlay" id="helpOverlay">
  <div class="modal wideModal">
    <h2>How to Play Word Havoc</h2>
    <div class="helpGrid">
      <div><b>1. Start at the center</b><span>Your opening word must cover the gold center medallion. After the first move, every word must connect to the existing board.</span></div><div><b>2. Attack</b><span>Build connected English words. Your WORD SCORE becomes damage, but Armor blocks damage before HP. Armor is your shield.</span></div>
      <div><b>3. HP is also money</b><span>Reforge 1 tile for 2 HP or 2–3 for 5 HP and keep your turn. An emergency full rerack costs 10 HP and ends it.</span></div>
      <div><b>4. Fight for battle squares</b><span>Green heals +5 HP, red doubles letter damage, and silver grants +8 Armor. Armor is a shield that gets used up before HP.</span></div>
      <div><b>5. React to the opponent</b><span>The opponent’s newest tiles glow gold. Build through them for Counterstrike damage. Crossings and captures add extra tactical damage.</span></div>
      <div><b>6. Two ways to win</b><span>Knock the opponent to 0 HP, or control 8 battle squares with a lead of 3+ and hold that Siege through their reply.</span></div>
      <div><b>7. Beat the clock</b><span>Standard turns are 60 seconds. Timing out forfeits the turn and burns 5 HP. Blitz is 30; Strategy is 90.</span></div>
    </div>
    <button class="btn primary" id="closeHelpBtn">Got It</button>
  </div>
</div>
